- **entrevista-win-loss**: Diseña y analiza entrevistas de ventas ganadas y perdidas con clientes potenciales que dijeron que no (y con clientes que dijeron que sí), extrayendo el motivo real de la decisión y convirtiéndolo en mejoras concretas. Úsalo cuando el usuario siga perdiendo ventas y sospeche que el motivo declarado no es el real. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/ventas-captar/agents/entrevista-win-loss.md))

- **epic-code-reviewer**: Adversarial code review. Reports every real issue, at most 10; zero is a valid result. Use for Phase 5 code-review workflow. ([Autopsias/gearbox-routing](https://github.com/Autopsias/gearbox-routing/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/epic-code-reviewer.md))
- **epic-conductor**: **Role:** Main agent for the epic plan flow. Handles interactive steps directly (requirements, summary, present), delegates milestone splitting and review to sub-agents. Focus: capture WHAT to build, break it into shippable phases. ([hunguyen1702/the-broke-workflow](https://github.com/hunguyen1702/the-broke-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/epic-conductor.md))
- **epic-controller**: An epic controller is a Claude session that owns one epic (`E&lt;n&gt;`) end to end. It plans, delegates each ticket to a coding agent through superorchestrator, reviews, merges into its epic branch and verifies. It does not write feature code itself. ([datamonsterr/duolify](https://github.com/datamonsterr/duolify/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/epic-controller.md))
- **epic-creator-agent**: You are the Epic Creator agent for GHPMplus. Your role is to analyze PRD (Product Requirements Document) issues and break them down into logical Epic-level groupings, creating properly structured GitHub issues. ([el-feo/ai-context](https://github.com/el-feo/ai-context/blob/HEAD/plugins/ghpmplus/agents/epic-creator-agent.md) · ★12)
- **epic-critic**: Critique one or two epic plans from a fixed stance (scope or approach), list the pros and cons of each, rebut the other critic, and gate the final plan. Spawned by the /dev-plan-epic skill; not for direct use. The prompt must include the stance, the round, the goal and the plans (and in later rounds the other critic's findings). ([synesenom/the-other-door](https://github.com/synesenom/the-other-door/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-critic.md) · ★1)
- **epic-decomposer**: You are an Agile Epic Architect. Your job is to decompose the project into well-bounded Epics with clear scope, dependencies, and sequencing. ([juliohokage/agile-ai-pipeline](https://github.com/juliohokage/agile-ai-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-decomposer.md))
- **epic-decomposer**: Breaks down epics into manageable user stories and tasks using INVEST principles, user journey analysis, dependency mapping, and adaptive learning from past decompositions ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/jira-epic-decomposer.md))
- **epic-deliverer**: Livre un lot d'un EPIC de Waterfall `en cours`, à partir de son issue : tire au besoin la branche de l'EPIC, confie le lot à l'agent de développement de son langage, enchaîne revue locale et correction jusqu'au vert, ouvre la pull request, la fusionne dans la branche de l'EPIC, clôt le lot et rend un relevé. Une instance par lot, lancée par le pilote (`docs/dev/agents.md`, « Le pilote »). Ne fusionne jamais dans `main`. ([waterfall-project/wf-project](https://github.com/waterfall-project/wf-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-deliverer.md) · ★2)
- **epic-delivery-orchestrator**: Orchestrate end-to-end epic delivery from Linear tasks intake to PR creation by delegating to specialist subagents, with a fast path for trivial changes done directly. ([trystan2k/tanstack-worker-template](https://github.com/trystan2k/tanstack-worker-template/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/epic-delivery-orchestrator.md))
- **epic-driver**: Drive one GitHub epic end-to-end via the drive-epic skill and the fleet (dispatch_smart). Not the default curriculum queue and not the infra lane. Started with ./start-claude.sh --epic N (or SESSION_EPIC=N). ([kube-dojo/kube-dojo.github.io](https://github.com/kube-dojo/kube-dojo.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-driver.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **epic-driver**: Retired Fleet role; retained for one version to explain migration. ([verkyyi/claude-fleet](https://github.com/verkyyi/claude-fleet/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet-epic/agents/epic-driver.md) · ★2)
- **epic-exit**: Epic-boundary check. Every ticket can pass while the epic still fails to deliver what SPEC 14.3 says it should. Verifies the epic's stated exit criterion against the running system. Always run before an epic merges to main. ([toddawhittaker/pulse-surveys](https://github.com/toddawhittaker/pulse-surveys/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-exit.md))
- **epic-explorer**: Explores one area of the repo for build-epic and writes one cited note; never edits the repo. ([rpvsilva/dotagents](https://github.com/rpvsilva/dotagents/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/epic-explorer.md) · 2 copies)
- **epic-explorer**: Reconnaissance agent for Epic Constraints authoring — surfaces decomposition candidates, refactor opportunities (foundational/opportunistic/deferred bins), and convention conflicts in the active repo's relevant subtree at epic altitude ([solventgrun/armature](https://github.com/solventgrun/armature/blob/HEAD/plugins/epic-design/agents/epic-explorer.md))
- **epic-framer**: Cadre un EPIC de Waterfall jusqu'à ses issues : détaille les US avec l'utilisateur, écrit la conception, établit le plan de lots, en s'arrêtant pour validation après chacune de ces trois étapes, puis ouvre les issues. À utiliser pour préparer un EPIC `à planifier` — souvent le suivant, pendant que le précédent se livre. Ne tire pas la branche de l'EPIC et n'écrit aucun code. Lancé « à blanc », rend la conception et le plan de lots qu'il proposerait, sans rien publier. ([waterfall-project/wf-project](https://github.com/waterfall-project/wf-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-framer.md) · ★2)
- **Epic-Gap-Resolver-Agent**: Complementary to Requirements-Intelligence-Agent. Reads gap assessment comments or pipeline JSON from JIRA epics, applies fixes to epic/story descriptions (missing AC, scope, KPIs, personas, dependencies, open questions), notifies epic and story owners about scope changes, and posts a resolution summary. Optionally triggers a re-score after fixes. Use when: 'Resolve gaps for PROJ-101', 'Apply critical fixes to epic PROJ-102', 'Update epic with missing AC from readiness assessment' ([AjinkaBhosale/claude-agent-fleet](https://github.com/AjinkaBhosale/claude-agent-fleet/blob/HEAD/agents/Epic-Gap-Resolver-Agent.md))
- **epic-graphs**: This project's only source for multi-role epic construction. A graph is guidance, not board data: `taskd` never reads it, nothing is stored or inherited. The recipe that instantiates a graph is the `meerkat` skill's `references/scenarios/epic.md`. ([tanyu216/meerkat-taskpanel](https://github.com/tanyu216/meerkat-taskpanel/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/epic-graphs.md) · ★1)
- **epic-group-worker**: Runs one lane of an epic — a sequential queue of tickets that owns a file territory no other lane touches. Used by the implement-epic skill, one instance per group, all spawned concurrently in isolated worktrees. It plans, implements, reviews and commits each ticket in its queue and pushes the branch chain; it never opens a PR and never merges to main. ([tareq89/biddaloy](https://github.com/tareq89/biddaloy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-group-worker.md))
- **epic-implementer**: Implements stories (TDD GREEN phase). Makes tests pass. Use for Phase 4 dev-story workflow. ([Autopsias/slashagents](https://github.com/Autopsias/slashagents/blob/HEAD/agents/epic-implementer.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **epic-implementer**: Implements a full CRM epic OR a single ticket from the JIRA backlog. For a full epic pass the epic key e.g. "implement EPIC-PLAT" or "implement EPIC-LEAD". For a single ticket pass the ticket ID e.g. "implement PLAT-1" or "implement LEAD-6". Writes code + tests, verifies AC and Unit Tests, auto-commits via hook, and produces a completion report. Do NOT use this agent for AUTH-* or NFR-* tickets — use epic-implementer-auth or epic-implementer-nfr instead. ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer.md))
- **epic-implementer**: Implementa una épica completa de Finlia (Laravel 13) siguiendo el protocolo de CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md. Úsalo cuando el usuario pida "implementa la épica N", "continúa con la épica X" o desarrolle una funcionalidad que pertenezca a una épica concreta. Lee el alcance en docs/ROADMAP.md (y la ficha en scrum/epics/ si la épica sigue abierta), inspecciona el código existente y aplica la regla de los 9 pasos. ([ronaldo-duran/Finlia](https://github.com/ronaldo-duran/Finlia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-implementer.md))
- **epic-implementer**: Builds one build-epic task test-first in its own worktree and commits on its task branch; never pushes or merges. ([rpvsilva/dotagents](https://github.com/rpvsilva/dotagents/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/epic-implementer.md) · 2 copies)
- **epic-implementer-acct**: Implements EPIC-ACCT only. Part of parallel group 4 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-lead, epic-implementer-cont, epic-implementer-pipe, epic-implementer-actv after AUTH completes. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-ACCT" or "Implement ACCT-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-acct.md))
- **epic-implementer-actv**: Implements EPIC-ACTV only. Part of parallel group 4 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-lead, epic-implementer-cont, epic-implementer-acct, epic-implementer-pipe after AUTH completes. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-ACTV" or "Implement ACTV-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-actv.md))
- **epic-implementer-anly**: Implements EPIC-ANLY only. Part of parallel group 8 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-camp and epic-implementer-comm after group 7 (PROJ, CONTR, INV) completes. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-ANLY" or "Implement ANLY-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-anly.md))
- **epic-implementer-auth**: Implements EPIC-AUTH tickets (AUTH-1 through AUTH-CHECK) with effort: high for deep reasoning on security-critical code — JWT, 2FA TOTP, RBAC, session management, password hashing. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-CONT" or "Implement CONT-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-cont.md))
- **epic-implementer-contr**: Implements EPIC-CONTR only. Part of parallel group 7 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-proj and epic-implementer-inv after INTG completes. - **epic-implementer-inv**: Implements EPIC-INV only. Part of parallel group 7 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-proj and epic-implementer-contr after INTG completes. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-INV" or "Implement INV-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-inv.md))
- **epic-implementer-kb**: Implements EPIC-KB only. Part of parallel group 5 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-tick and epic-implementer-sla after group 4 (LEAD, CONT, ACCT, PIPE, ACTV) completes. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-KB" or "Implement KB-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-kb.md))
- **epic-implementer-lead**: Implements EPIC-LEAD only. Part of parallel group 4 — run simultaneously with epic-implementer-cont, epic-implementer-acct, epic-implementer-pipe, epic-implementer-actv after AUTH completes. Invoke with "Implement EPIC-LEAD" or "Implement LEAD-N". ([PooraniBala/AntiClock](https://github.com/PooraniBala/AntiClock/blob/HEAD/Documents/Claude/.claude/agents/epic-implementer-lead.md))
- **epic-implementer-nfr**: Implements EPIC-NFR tickets (NFR-1 through NFR-CHECK) with effort: high for deep reasoning on performance, scalability, accessibility, and compliance requirements. - **epic-integrator**: Worker — merges component branches in dependency order, resolves conflicts by intent, and runs the full build and test suite ([zb-ss/claude-plugin-workflow](https://github.com/zb-ss/claude-plugin-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/epic-integrator.md) · ★1)
- **epic-lead**: Runs an epic end to end. Turns a goal into specced tasks with acceptance criteria and a wave plan, dispatches workers, holds shared-resource locks, catches agents going in circles, and signs off PRs before the human merges. Use for anything spanning more than one task. ([vladokuskov/software-factory](https://github.com/vladokuskov/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/epic-lead.md))
- **epic-orchestration**: How one agent takes an epic from "open issues" to "merged on `main`", running developers and reviewers in parallel and escalating to the human only where a human is genuinely needed. ([luciocabrera/lcabrera-stack](https://github.com/luciocabrera/lcabrera-stack/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/epic-orchestration.md))
- **epic-orchestrator**: L0 Epic Orchestrator that manages multiple roadmaps. Analyzes scope, creates EPIC.json with roadmap placeholders, spawns Roadmap Orchestrators sequentially, and gates progress. ([evan043/claude-cli-advanced-starter-pack](https://github.com/evan043/claude-cli-advanced-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/epic-orchestrator.template.md) · ★62)
- **epic-orchestrator**: Drives one GitHub issue's sections through code-issue → review-issue → pr-from-issue → merge-pr, each step a spawned subagent in the section's own worktree, independent sections in parallel, one wave per invocation. Reads the human review labels, never writes them. Never spawns improve-issue: returns STATUS: SPEC_PENDING instead. Invoked by `orchestrate-issue`, not by a user.
